## CW-002 — CW-1 implementation and pre-commit review repairs

- **Date/time:** 2026-08-10 19:32:41 -05:00 (`America/Chicago`).
- **State:** uncommitted CW-1 working tree on base/parent `e723c509f65fc82abbeafba625071207425892e0`; no push, pull request, deployment, live database, live data, customer action, or other outward action occurred.
- **Implementation:** Weather retains the original **Open blank spray record** path and adds the separate **Start spray record with this weather** action only while the forecast is actionably fresh. The new versioned route contract copies and freezes exactly field UUID, forecast-local date, temperature, wind speed, and one eight-point compass direction. Inventory validates the route state, validates the field against the current farm workspace, opens the existing Spray record form, and leaves every value editable. Crop, acres, time, pest, applicator, license, humidity, and product facts remain blank/unprefilled. No migration, provenance record, provider link, background write, or new repository save path was added.
- **Explicit-save boundary:** route creation, navigation, form initialization, edits, abandonment, and stale-weather rendering contain no repository/database write. Only the existing **Save spray record** path can persist an application and perform its existing completed-application Inventory draw-down. Successful confirmed or queued saves replace the browser history entry with null route state so reload cannot reapply an accepted handoff. Failed or needs-attention saves retain the farmer's values for review.
- **Pre-commit Sol review findings:** a bounded read-only Sol High review found four P2 issues and no P1 issue: consumed prefill lived only in React state and could return on reload; forecast freshness was not re-evaluated when an open page crossed the two-hour ceiling; same-path route-state changes could reuse old controlled/uncontrolled form values; and the parser accepted numbers beyond the destination database bounds.
- **Repairs:** successful/queued consumption now uses history `replace`; Weather schedules an expiry re-render and rechecks freshness in the click handler; the Spray form remounts on each route location key; and route plus form bounds match the database contract (`-100..160` °F, `0..250` mph). Focused malformed-state regressions cover exact-key rejection, invalid UUID/date/direction, non-finite numbers, out-of-range values, and the payload-free manual intent.
- **Changed-file allowlist at this state:** `src/InventoryModule.tsx`; `src/WeatherModule.tsx`; `src/data/weatherNoWrite.regression.ts`; `src/data/weatherSprayHandoff.regression.ts`; `src/data/weatherSprayHandoff.ts`; `tests/e2e/season/cedar-creek.spec.ts`; `playwright.connect-workflows-cw1.config.ts`; `scripts/verify-connect-workflows-cw1-disposable.ps1`; `tests/season/connect-workflows-cw1.verify.sql`; and this ledger. No Grain, Harvest, bin, contract, delivery, migration, package, lockfile, fixture, repository, or live-system file changed.
- **Current gate:** **IMPLEMENTED; VERIFICATION IN PROGRESS.**

## CW-007 — Correction: Harvest passed unmodified and Cedar's pre-browser harness failure was repaired

- **Date/time:** 2026-08-11 05:27:17 -05:00 (`America/Chicago`).
- **Correction to CW-006 Harvest gate:** after V2's final clean release, root independently re-proved clean topology and ran the original, unmodified `scripts/verify-harvest-ridge-disposable.ps1` exactly once. It exited `0` in 1,216.2 seconds. All canonical HR1-HR5, extension-bin, extension-in, extension-contract, phone, exact SQL, reverse HR5/HR4, clock-phase, and final `HARVEST_RIDGE_2027_DISPOSABLE_PASS` markers passed. The earlier HR2 failure was environmental. No Harvest/Grain/bin/contract/delivery file was changed, no fence exception is required, and the Mason decision requested in CW-006 is withdrawn.
- **Read-only overlap disclosure:** while CW owned the backend and Harvest was running, V2 mistakenly invoked `scripts/harvest-ridge-db-clock.regression.ps1` after classifying it as offline. V2 reported that it performed only read-only `docker inspect` calls against the ordinary database/rest container names, then failed its expected-topology assertion and stopped. It did not reset, restart, write, launch a browser/scenario, or create/remove a listener, container, journal, or image. Root adjudicated the overlap as non-mutating; it does not invalidate Harvest's write/non-write result or CW's exclusive mutation ownership. V2 committed to zero further backend/topology calls until CW releases.
- **Cedar primary failure captured:** a direct unmodified rerun emitted `CEDAR_CREEK_2027_STATIC_CONTRACT_PASS`, reset successfully, began the Playwright launch, then exited `1` in 113 seconds. The first native output was the routine npm notice for `npx`; under the wrapper's global `$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'`, merged native stderr became a terminating `NativeCommandError`/`ParentContainsErrorRecordException`. The clock adapter rethrew that primary exception at line 131 before the harness could print its captured Playwright output or exit marker. This explains CW's and both V2 Cedar failures; they were harness-output failures, not credited browser assertion failures.
- **Narrow Cedar repair:** only `scripts/verify-cedar-creek-disposable.ps1` changed. Around the native Playwright capture it saves the prior error-action preference, uses `Continue` so routine native stderr can be captured, and restores the prior strict preference in `finally`. The existing explicit nonzero-exit and failed-summary checks remain unchanged. The action also reads the existing verifier SQL before the clock phase and requires its exact `CEDAR_CREEK_2027_VERIFY_PASS` marker through direct local `psql`, retaining V2 repair provenance while avoiding function lookup across the frozen clock boundary. No product, fixture, migration, assertion, expected write/non-write, or protected module changed.
- **Cedar focused proof:** StaticOnly contract, PowerShell parse, and `git diff --check` passed. The repaired full disposable packet then exited `0` in 226.2 seconds: desktop Playwright `1 passed`, `CEDAR_CREEK_PLAYWRIGHT_EXIT:desktop:0`; phone Playwright `1 passed`, `CEDAR_CREEK_PLAYWRIGHT_EXIT:phone:0`; exact SQL accepted after each viewport; and `CEDAR_CREEK_2027_DISPOSABLE_PASS` emitted. Recovery left zero clock journals, clock images, parked containers, or governed listeners and a healthy ordinary database.

- **Exact diagnosis and smallest proof-only repair:** PostgreSQL documents that `dblink_get_result` can block until a remote query completes and that cancellation is only a request.
